# OgVPN Master UI/UX + Demo + Real VPN Production-Readiness Checklist — 2,500 Items

> **MARKED EXECUTION COPY — generated by scripts/mark_checklist.py**
> Tiers: RRV = real runtime verified (evidence captured) · ATV = automated tests ·
> BV = CI build verified · IMP = implemented, not runtime-verified · NVE = needs real device/VPS.
> Only RRV/ATV/BV are ticked. Unticked items carry an explicit blocker.


## Mandatory execution law
Treat this as an execution checklist, not a documentation exercise. Use INSPECT → UNDERSTAND → PLAN → IMPLEMENT → RUN → INSPECT ACTUAL RESULT → TEST → DIAGNOSE → FIX → RETEST → REGRESSION → VERIFY → DELIVER.
Never claim completion from source existence or compilation alone. For every item record whether it is IMPLEMENTED, BUILD VERIFIED, AUTOMATED TEST VERIFIED, or REAL RUNTIME VERIFIED, plus evidence.
Demo mode must be explicitly labeled and must never claim a real VPN tunnel. Real mode must be verified against an actual VPN server, actual tunnel state, actual traffic, DNS/routing, disconnect, and recovery.

## Required coverage cross-check before delivery

- [ ] Confirm the 2,500 items collectively cover accessibility, visual hierarchy, typography, spacing, responsive behavior, scroll ownership, menus, drawers, dialogs, forms, loading, empty, error, success, offline, recovery, and keyboard/focus behavior.
- [ ] Confirm the 2,500 items collectively cover Android VpnService, VPN permissions, foreground service behavior, tunnel lifecycle, network transitions, sleep/wake, process death, notifications, and actual traffic.
- [ ] Confirm the 2,500 items collectively cover Windows, macOS, and Linux desktop behavior wherever each platform is declared supported.
- [ ] Confirm the 2,500 items collectively cover web authentication, session persistence, protected routes, devices, servers, settings, billing, and production deployment.
- [ ] Confirm the 2,500 items collectively cover extension lifecycle, permissions, authentication, storage, updates, and uninstall behavior.
- [ ] Confirm the 2,500 items collectively cover API authorization, validation, idempotency, timeout, retry, stale state, concurrency, migrations, backup, restore, security, and observability.
- [ ] Confirm the 2,500 items collectively cover DNS, IPv4, IPv6, routing, split tunneling, kill switch, leak prevention, and failure recovery.
- [ ] Confirm GitHub Actions performs clean builds, tests, artifact generation, security checks, and cross-platform release builds.
- [ ] Confirm every discovered defect receives root-cause repair and regression verification.
- [ ] Do not declare production-ready while any critical/high defect remains or while any real-VPN claim lacks runtime evidence.
